Potential low-dimensional continuous optimization can find M<0.75 if exists. Let's design a reliable continuous optimizer:
- Use a grid of Ns points (e.g., 512 or 1024). Let p_i be nonnegative, sum=1.
- Objective: max convolution q_i (computed via FFT or direct). We can minimize smooth surrogate: L = sum exp(β q_i) or log-sum-exp plus constraints p_i≥0.
- Optimize with gradient descent in log p: p_i ∝ exp(θ_i), θ unconstrained, normalization. Gradient of L wrt θ: grad_i = p_i * dL/dp_i - (dL/dθ·p) to enforce sum? Let's derive: q=sum p p. d/dθ_l p_i = δ_il p_i - p_i p_l (if normalize). Use projected/subgradient. Simpler: use multiplicative updates with penalty H = ∑ w_k (q_k)^2 or exp.

For smooth objective f(p)=∑ exp(β q_k)/β (approx max). q_k = ∑ p_i p_{k-i}. Gradient wrt p_i = (2/β)∑_k exp(β q_k) p_{k-i}. Under constraint sum p=1, project gradient: p_i <- p_i - η (grad_i - mean grad?) Actually for log transform, parameter θ with p=softmax(θ). Gradient wrt θ: ∂f/∂θ_i = ∑_j ∂f/∂p_j (δ_ij - p_j)p_i = p_i(∂f/∂p_i - ∂f/∂p·p). Update θ_i -= η gradient_i. This stays positive. For β large, active q. This is promising and low-dim; for high-dim maybe too.

We can optimize n=2535 directly with this log-softmax multiplicative gradient. It's essentially smoothing. Need choose β, step. Could converge to local minima. Starting from random/logistic may work. Combine with noise/annealing.

But direct objective is max, not convex. However optimizing exp surrogate with β large is nonconvex? f=∑ exp(β q_k) is not convex due to q indefinite; gradient may oscillate.

Potential robust local search: Point-update with exact convolution update.
For each coordinate i changing a_i→a_i' (then renormalize sum), objective change can be computed quickly? Convolution changes Δc_k = a_i' - a_i times partners. If coordinate value changes from old to new (and adjust all coordinates to maintain sum? Hard). Without rescale, scale invariant; we can just allow scaling. We can use multiplicative updates on individual coordinates and compute full convolution each time O(n²). Coordinate descent maybe 1 coefficient, but full conv each O6.5M; 2500 coords * many too slow.

Block updates: change a block of width w; update convolution for all lags affected: O((n+2w)w) if using direct difference: new_conv = old_conv + Δ_outer with partners. Then max via np.max O(n). For w small (10), O(nw)=25k, much faster than full conv 6.5M. We can do local search with block multiplicative perturbations using incremental convolution. This is powerful. We can call evaluate_sequence occasionally for authoritative local improvements, but use internal objective.

Implement incremental convolution update:
old = a. new = a copy. changed indices mask [start:stop]. We can compute delta = new - old. Need add contribution to c for all k involving at least one changed index:
For each changed i and every j (0..n-1), c_{i+j} += delta_i * old_j + old_i * delta_j - delta_i*delta_j? But if multiple changed, easier recompute from scratch for affected? Could do full convolution of delta with a plus delta with delta? This is O(nw) if delta sparse width w: np.convolve(delta, a) O(nw) and np.convolve(delta,delta) O(w²) if we implement direct over changed. But np.convolve of sparse delta length n still O(n²) if dense? We can manually loop over changed indices and vector a: c += delta_i * a shifted twice (for symmetric product) plus interactions. Since only one block, O(w n).
Detailed: a_new = a + d (d nonzero at block). c_new = c_old + ∑_{i in B} δ_i ( a_i*(a shifted?) ) ... Derive:
c(k)=∑_i a_i a_{k-i}.
Let B. Contribution involving at least one B:
∑_{i∈B} δ_i a_{k-i} + ∑_{j∈B} a_{k-j} δ_j? Because changed both i and k-i. Plus for both in B: -∑_{i,j∈B} δ_i a? Wait expand:
a_i a_{k-i} = (old_i+d_i)(old_{k-i}+d_{k-i}) = old_i old_{k-i} + d_i old_{k-i} + old_i d_{k-i} + d_i d_{k-i}.
Sum over i. Terms: old conv + ∑_i d_i old_{k-i} + ∑_i old_i d_{k-i} + ∑_i d_i d_{k-i}.
The first two are d*a and a*d (convolutions) but with valid bounds. Since d sparse, compute for each changed i:
- term1: d_i * old_{k-i} for k=i+old_index valid: add to c[i + j] where j=0..n-1: d_i * old_j.
- term2: old_i * d_{k-i}: for changed k-i=j -> k=i+old? Actually d_{k-i} at changed j, so c_{i+j} += old_i * d_j. (This is a*d).
- term3: d_i d_{k-i}: c_{i+j} += d_i d_j for i,j∈B.
Term1 and term2 are symmetric; if we do for each changed i add d_i*old shifted from i, and old_i*d shifted from i, plus pair triple. For vectorized:
delta = a_new - old.
support = indices with nonzero.
For each i in support:
    c[i:i+n] += delta[i]*old
    c += old[i]*delta shifted starting at i? Need c[i + j] += old[i]*delta[j]. That's add old[i]*delta to c[i:].
These two operations O(w n) in Python loops w times (w maybe 20) -> 50k vector ops, okay.
Then pair term O(w²). We can skip pair if delta small? Need exact.

For block perturbations, width maybe 10-100. O(w n) each; w=50 ->126k adds, Python 50 loops -> maybe 0.5ms? Actually each vector add length n (2500) -> 50*2*2500=250k float ops, Python overhead 100 loops, maybe 0.1ms? Fine. Could do many local moves.

Local search move types:
1. Multiplicative block: choose start,length, factor e^{eta * normal or cos pattern}. Apply to values.
2. Rebalancing move: choose two blocks, transfer mass: decrease block1 factor f, increase block2 factor 1/f' while preserving sum? Or adjust values with additive? To maintain nonneg, use multiplicative with zero issues; can clip and rescale.
3. Swap/permutation moves: reorder a subset of values. Since objective depends on positions, permuting high values can be effective. We can sort? Maybe choose a block and shift its values by d positions. Convolution update can be computed from scratch if block changed. Local search with swaps can reduce additive collisions.
4. Block shear: apply a smooth ramp factor exp(eta*x) across a block, making gradient.

But high dimension local minima could be many. We need global. We can combine with periodic large restarts and simulated annealing.

Let's attempt to find continuous p via solving first-order conditions. At optimum with max q=M over active set A. Lagrangian L=∑λ_k(q_k-M) - ν(∑p-1), λ_k≥0. For p_i>0: ∑_k λ_k p_{k-i} = ν/2 = const. - Move types:

  * two_blocks: choose two blocks, multiply block1 by e^η, block2 by e^{-η} (relative transfer). With rescale, both affected; but roughly transfer.

  * block reverse: reverse values in block.
  * block normalization: set block to its mean plus noise? Could smooth.

Pseudo:
def update_conv_block(a, c, start, stop, new_block):
    old_block = a[start:stop].copy()
    delta = new_block - old_block
    # Add terms:
    # For each idx, val in delta:
    for pos, dv in enumerate(delta):
        i = start + pos
        if dv != 0:
            c[i:i+n] += dv * a_old? Wait a is being updated? We need old a for shifts. Use old_a = a (before modification) for both old_i and old_j.
But if we update in place after computing all contributions, we need old values. We can store old_block. For term1 and term2 using old a:
    for pos, dv:
        i=start+pos
        c[i:i+n] += dv * old_a       # d_i * old_j
    for pos, old_i:
        i=start+pos
        c[i:i+n] += old_i * delta[pos] # old_i*d_j
    # pair term:
    for pos_i, di:
        for pos_j,dj:
            c[start+pos_i+start+pos_j] += di*dj
Then a[start:stop]=new_block.
This is exact if c was exact. Note pair term includes i,j all with d_i d_j at k=i+j. Since c updated by linear terms using old a, and pair term. Good.
Double counting: term1 sum_i d_i old_{k-i}; term2 sum_i old_i d_{k-i}; For k where both changed? old terms include contributions from changed old values; pair adds new-new with old-old difference? Let's verify: new = old +d. new conv = old conv + sum_i [d_i old_{k-i} + old_i d_{k-i} + d_i d_{k-i}]. If we use old_i for term2 at changed i, and old_a for old_{k-i} in term1, correct. Pair term includes d_i,d_k-i. For changed pairs (i,j), when i or j changed, d_i d_j counted. Need all i,j in support; loop O(w²) at k=i+j. Good.
Term1 and term2 for each changed index add vector length n. Can vectorize with broadcasting if block delta not too small? For a block, we can create delta_ext = delta[:,None]? We need add for each i: c[i:i+n] += dv_i * old_a. This can be done by constructing a matrix? Not memory. Loop w times. w up to maybe 100, okay. For many moves, Python loop overhead but manageable. We can optimize by limiting w <= 64.

Need handle indices beyond c length: c length 2n-1. For i from0 to n-1, c[i:i+n] length n. Good.

This update assumes no clipping of a. New_block must be nonnegative; we can clip to 0. If some a values become negative due to floating, clip.

Normalization: We want preserve sum1. If new_block sum changes, after computing c for new unscaled a, let new_S = S + (sum_new_block - sum_old_block). If new_S <=0, reject. scale = S / new_S. Then a[start:stop] *= scale? Actually entire a? We can avoid scaling all a if S fixed? For future exact score, we need a sum1. Scaling all a by scale would change whole c by scale² and a all entries. If we scale all a, cmax scaled. Good.

But if we scale all a every move, c remains consistent with a, and S stays constant (floating drift maybe). We can periodically renormalize exactly.

Let's design small-scale SOTA LP function:
`run_sota_lp(p, n, rng, max_time, max_iter)` returns best p (sum1) and score. It works with S=sqrt(2n) but we can store sum1; convert inside. We can call `linprog` if scipy available. If not, fallback.

Detailed:
from scipy.optimize import linprog
def _lp_update(f, M, rng):
    n=f.size
    # Build A
    A = np.zeros((2*n-1, n), dtype=np.float64)
    for j in range(n): A[j:j+n, j] = f
    # To avoid degeneracy and ensure b=f feasible? f is feasible if f*f <= M. Numerical. Add small tolerance.
    bounds = [(0, None)]*n
    ccoef = -np.ones(n)
    # Perturb objective slightly: ccoef -= 1e-9 * rng.random(n) maybe to pick vertex.
    res = linprog(ccoef, A_ub=A, b_ub=np.full(2*n-1, M*(1+1e-9)), bounds=bounds, method='highs')
    if res.success and res.x is not None:
       b=res.x
    else: # fallback simple
    Return b.

But linprog may be very slow building A each update. For n=128 trivial. For n=512, A 524k? Actually 1023*512=523k, small. For n=1024, 1M. We can use n up to512 maybe.

LP objective may return b with sum 0 if infeasible? b=0 always feasible, but f feasible, so optimum positive. If numerical infeasible due to M slightly low, use b=f.

Then B=sum(b). If B<=0, b=f. g=S/B*b. line search t in f->g:
- f_s = S/n? f normalized S=sqrt(2n). Let f_det normalized S. M-f = max conv.
- For candidate g sum S.
- Define h(t)=max(conv((1-t)f+t g)). We can approximate? Need evaluate conv for a few t. Use binary search t in (0, t_max) with exact conv for chosen t. Since n small, cheap. Try t from [0.5,0.25,0.1,0.05,0.01] decreasing until h(t)<=M+tol. If none, maybe accept minimal increase? SOTA says nonincreasing; we can require not increase. If small increase, could still help global but stick.
- Update f = (1-t)f+t g, M updated. If t too tiny, maybe skip.
- Additionally, regularize f to positive: f = np.clip(f, eps, None); normalize S.
LP may produce g with zeros; blend t small keeps positive.
Repeat until time or no improvement for many iterations. Track best M (score) of f (since S fixed). Also evaluate g? maybe after blend.

Will SOTA LP from random f reduce? It solves b with constraints f*b<=M. If M current, f itself feasible. If f random, M maybe >? The LP max sum b likely >S, g has sum S but maybe more "anti-correlated". Line search should reduce M. Good.

We can run for N=64,128,256,512 with several starts. Generating random starts: p = exp(rng.normal(0, sigma)); normalize; also linear, stepped, parent interpolated.

High-res local search details:
Input p sum1 length n. c full conv. deadline. best internal.
Basic loop:
    while time < deadline:
       # choose move type
       if rng.random() < 0.2: global_smooth_move (full conv)
       else: local_block_move (incremental)

       after every X moves reconvolve to avoid drift.
       print progress.

Local block move function:
- `start = rng.integers(0, n)`, `width = int(rng.integers(min_w, max_w))`. Ensure stop<=n.
- Generate factor array length w. Options:
   1. uniform: factor = exp(eta * rng.normal(0,1))
   2. ramp: x=np.linspace(-1,1,w); factor = exp(eta * (x if rng<0.5 else -x))
   3. wave: phase=rng.random()*2π; factor = exp(eta * np.sin(2π/3*x + phase))? But w small.
   4. two-tone: random split alpha: factor[:k]=exp(eta), factor[k:]=exp(-eta)
- new_block = a[start:stop] * factor; clip to >=0. To avoid all zero, if sum_new_block too small, reject.
- Apply update, normalize all a to sum1.
Potential issue: if block contains zeros, multiplying remains zero; cannot increase zeros. To allow mass into zeros, use additive perturbation: new_block = a_block * factor + small_amount? If p_i=epsilon, multiplication increases slightly, not much. Use move `new_block = (1-overwrite)*a_block + overwrite*rng.uniform(0, local_mean?)` to create values. For block shift/reverse, values permute so no zeros issue.

Full global smooth move:
- Choose pattern φ_i from basis:
   linear: linspace(-1,1,n) times sign
   quadratic: (2x-1)^2 -1 (negative center)

- factor = exp(eta * φ_i). new_a = a*factor, normalize, compute full conv. Accept if improves or annealed.
This can adjust global shape. eta smaller (0.2-0.8).

Maybe better: run deterministic hill climbing with random perturbations; if stuck (no improvement for K moves), do a larger "kick" (randomly perturb a large block or restart from best + noise). Pseudo local_search:
cur = p.copy(); c=conv
for move in range(...):
  choose eta,width based on progress maybe anneal since last kick.
  generate new_a; if full: c_new=conv(new_a); score_new=...
  elif block: update; score_new;
  if score_new <= cur_score:
      accept; cur_score=...
      if cur_score < best_score: best=cur
      stuck=0
      eta *= decay_good? maybe keep
  else:
      stuck +=1
      # occasional accept with prob temp
      if score_new - cur_score < temp and rng.random()<0.1: accept
  if stuck > 2000:
      # kick: strong random perturbation of current or best
      cur = add_noise(best, amplitude kick_amp)
      c=conv(cur); cur_score=score; eta reset larger
      stuck=0
  every 5000 recompute c from a to correct.
Time loop.

Kicks: start from best (not current) to avoid drifting. Add noise: `cur = best * exp(rng.normal(0,kick_sigma,n)); normalize; c=conv`. kick_sigma start 0.5, anneal to0.05. Also with prob, use a random pattern. This explores.

Splitting function:
def split_sequence(v, rng, noise=0.1):
  nv = np.empty(2*v.size)
  for i,val in enumerate(v):
      # desired sum val; choose ratio r ~ lognormal small
      r = float(np.exp(rng.normal(0, noise)))
      a = val/2 * r; b=val/2 / r
      nv[2i]=a; nv[2i+1]=b
  return nv/sum
This preserves sum and gradually adds degree.

Move implementations detail:
`full_smooth_move(a, n, rng, eta)`:
  x = np.linspace(-1,1,n)
  type = rng.integers(0,4)
  if type0: phi = x
  type1: phi = x*x - 0.5
  type2: f = rng.integers(1,6); phi = np.sin(2π f (x+1)/2 + phase)
  type3: phi = np.cos(2π f ... )
  factor = np.exp(eta * phi)
  new_a = a * factor
  s = new_a.sum()
  if s>0: new_a/=s
  else reject
  return new_a
Full conv: c = np.convolve(new_a,new_a); score=2n max(c). Accept.

Potential bug in incremental convolution: We use old_a after converting to new? Implementation carefully:
```
def update_conv_block(a, c, start, stop, new_block, n):
    old_block = a[start:stop].copy()
    delta = new_block - old_block
    # linear terms using old a (before modification)
    for idx in range(start, stop):
        dv = delta[idx-start]
        if dv != 0.0:
            c[idx:idx+n] += dv * a  # a is old
    for idx in range(start, stop):
        oi = a[idx]
        dv = delta[idx-start]
        if dv != 0.0:
            c[idx:idx+n] += oi * dv
    # pair term
    for ii in range(start, stop):
        di = delta[ii-start]
        if di ==0: continue
        base = ii
        for jj in range(start, stop):
            dj = delta[jj-start]
            if dj !=0:
                c[base+jj] += di*dj
    a[start:stop] = new_block
```
Need term2: `oi * d_j` where c index = oi_index + k-i? Wait term2 derived: ∑_i old_i d_{k-i}. Let j = k-i = changed index; then k = i+j. So c[i+j] += old_i * d_j. The code `for idx in changed i: for changed j?` The vectorized `c[idx:idx+n] += old_block[pos] * delta` adds to c[idx + j] for j=0..n-1? But j runs over all indices, but delta is nonzero only at changed positions. `delta` as full array? If delta is block length and we add to c[idx:idx+n] += old_i * delta_full? We don't have delta_full aligned: For j from 0..n-1, d_j = delta at j if j in block else 0. If we do `c[idx:idx+n] += old_i * delta_full` where delta_full length n with zeros outside block, works. But in block loop, using block delta vector of length w added to c[idx:idx+n] would incorrectly assume j relative 0 maps to global j=idx? Need careful.

Term2 vectorized per j? Let's rethink. Term1: ∑_{i changed} d_i old_{k-i}. For fixed i, k = i + j, j=0..n-1. c[i:i+n] += d_i * old_a. This is correct for all j.
Term2: ∑_{i changed} old_i d_{k-i}. For fixed i (changed), k-i = j where j must be changed. We can sum over j changed: c[i+j] += old_i * d_j. This is sparse pair-like, O(w²). Not a vector of length n unless delta_full. We can combine term1 and term2 differently:
Alternative: New c = old c + d*a + a*d + d*d (convolutions). Here d*a and a*d are full convolutions of sparse d with old a. `d*a` term: for each changed i, c[i+j] += d_i old_j. That's term1. `a*d`: for each changed j, c[i+j] += old_i d_j. This is for each changed j, add d_j * old_a shifted starting at j: c[j:j+n] += d_j * old_a. Ah! Term2 can be vectorized the same as term1 but using changed index as the j index: For each changed j, c[j:j+n] += d_j * old_a. Let's verify coefficient: a*d at k = ∑_i old_i d_{k-i}; let j=k-i changed, i=k-j: ∑_j d_j old_{k-j}; for fixed j, k=j+i -> c[j+i] += old_i d_j = d_j * old_a at j+i. Yes. So both linear terms are: for each changed index j, c[j:j+n] += d_j * old_a (two times? Wait d*a and a*d are identical if convolution commutative. Since convolution a*a symmetric, the sum d*a + a*d is just 2*(d*a) for commutative? Let's check: (d*a)_k = ∑_i d_i old_{k-i}; (old*d)_k =∑_i old_i d_{k-i}. Pseudo `local_search`:

def local_search(initial, n, rng, deadline, verbose=False):
    a = np.asarray(initial).copy(); if size !=n: adjust; normalize
    c = _convolution(a); cur_score = _score(a,c,n)
    best_a = a.copy(); best_score=cur_score
    start_time=monotonic(); elapsed_full? We'll use deadlines.
    moved=0; accepted=0; stuck=0
    # maybe set initial temp
    while monotonic() < deadline - 2:
       total_elapsed = monotonic()-start_time
       # maybe max runtime fraction to get progress even if deadline far? Use elapsed for annealing; if deadline 2000s, progress okay.
       prog = min(1.0, total_elapsed / max(1e-6, deadline-start_time)) if deadline>start else 0
       eta = 0.8*(0.03/0.8)**prog
       wmax = ...
       r = rng.random()
       if r < 0.1:
          # full smooth
          new_a = _global_smooth(a,n,rng,eta*0.5)
          c_new=conv(new_a); score_new=2n max(c_new)
          if _accept(score_new,cur_score,prog,rng): accept full
       else if r<0.25:
          # block permute
          try ... block_permute update
       else:
          # block factor
       if accept:
           cur_s=score_new; if cur_s < best_score: best...
           moved++; accepted++; stuck=0
       else:
           stuck+=1
       if moved % 5000 ==0:
           reconvolve exact
           print if verbose
       if stuck > stuck_limit (depending on n maybe 1000):
           kick
    return best_a,best_score
```
Accept function:
```
def should_accept(old,new,prog,rng):
   if new <= old: return True
   diff=new-old
   temp = 1e-6*(1-prog)+1e-9 # score units
   if diff <= 0.0: True
   if diff < 20*temp and rng.random() < np.exp(-diff/temp): return True
   return False
```
Maybe more aggressive: temp = max(1e-7, 1e-5*(1-prog)). But near optimum, differences small (1e-6). Use exp.

Set `stuck_limit = 2000` for large, maybe 500 for small. If no improvement for many, kick.
Kick sigma: `kick_sigma = 0.35*(1-prog)+0.05`.
From best:
```
base = best_a.copy()
noise = np.exp(rng.normal(0,kick_sigma,n))
a = base*noise; normalize; c=full; cur_score=2n max; stuck=0
```
Sometimes kick with a new random smooth global pattern or random permutation of best? But noise enough.

def hierarchical_build(max_n, deadline, rng):
    seqs=[]
    current = np.array([1.0])
    # record len1 score 2 (not useful)
    levels = [2,4,8,16,32,64,128,256,512,1024,2048,4096]
    times = {2:1,4:1,8:2,16:4,32:8,64:15,128:30,256:60,512:90,1024:120,2048:120,4096:120}
    for B in levels:
       if B > max_n: break? If B>max_n, we can still develop small sequence B? For upsample down? Not. If hierarchy length > final, we could use as high-res direct (length 4096) but return maybe length 4096. Allowed? Sequence length arbitrary. Could be beneficial? Larger n maybe better but search time larger. We can choose max_n = max(n_final, 2048) maybe. Actually if B=4096, local search length4096 still fine; could find better than 2535. But target parent 2535; length 4096 may score lower. Let's include B up to 2048 to keep. If max_n smaller, stop.
       # level time from times; but ensure not exceed deadline and leave final. We can scale based on remaining?
       level_deadline = min(deadline, monotonic()+times[B])
       new = split_sequence(current, rng, noise=0.15)
       best_p, best_score = local_search(new, B, rng, level_deadline, verbose=False)
       current = best_p
       seqs.append((best_p, B, best_score))
       if monotonic() >= deadline: break
    return seqs
```

Need implement exact normalization: 
```
def normalize(a):
    s = a.sum()
    if not finite or s<=0: return np.ones(len(a))/len(a), False
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=float)
    a = np.maximum(a,0)
    return a/s, True
```
When carrying a, ensure sum exactly1: after local acceptance scaling, due to floating sum maybe not exactly; fine.

Full score function: 
```
def sequence_score_full(a):
   n=a.size; if n==0 return inf
   S=a.sum(); if S<=0 return inf
   c=np.convolve(a,a)
   S2=S*S
   return 2*n*np.max(c)/S2
```
Since we normalize, S=1.

```
import time
import numpy as np
try: from scipy.optimize import linprog
except: linprog=None

def _finite_array(x): ...
def _normalize(a, force=True): ...
def _convolve(a): return np.convolve(a.astype(np.float64), a.astype(np.float64))
def _score_from_c(c,n,S=1.0): ...
def _score_full(a): n=a.size; S=a.sum(); if S<1e-12 return inf; c=_convolve(a/S); return 2*n*np.max(c)
```

"""Hierarchical multiresolution local search..."""
import time, numpy as np
try: from scipy.optimize import linprog
except Exception: linprog=None

def _get_parent(): ...

def _try_block_update(a,c,n,start,stop,new_block): ... returns success, score (and updates a,c) or restores.
def _block_permute_move(...): similar
def _local_search(initial, n, rng, deadline, verbose=False): ...

Detailed `_local_search`:
```
def _local_search(initial, n, rng, deadline, verbose=False):
    a = np.array(initial, dtype=float).copy()
    if a.size != n:
       a = _interpolate(a, n)
    a, ok = _normalize(a)
    if not ok:
       a=np.ones(n)/n
    c = _conv(a)
    cur_score = _score_c(c,n)
    best_a = a.copy(); best_score = cur_score
    if verbose: print("local start n",n,"score",cur_score)
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    total_time = max(1e-9, deadline - t0)
    moved=0; acts=0; stuck=0; last_print=t0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline - 1.0:
        elapsed = time.monotonic()-t0
        prog = min(1.0, elapsed/total_time)
        eta = 0.8 * (0.03/0.8)**prog
        # move type
        if n <= 64: full_prob=0.3; perm_prob=0.2
        else: full_prob=0.12; perm_prob=0.15
        r = rng.random()
        accepted=False
        if r < full_prob:
            new_a = _global_smooth(a, n, rng, eta*0.6)
            if new_a is not None:
                c_new = _conv(new_a)
                score_new = _score_c(c_new,n)
                if _accept(move_score_new, cur_score, prog, rng):
                    a=new_a; c=c_new; cur_score=score_new; accepted=True
        else:
            # choose width
            if n <= 64:
                wlo=1; whi=max(2,n//2)
            else:
                wlo=max(2, min(16, n//200))
                whi=max(wlo+1, min(n//5, 128))
            width=int(rng.integers(wlo, whi+1))
            if width >= n:
                width=n
            start=int(rng.integers(0, max(1, n-width+1)))
            stop=start+width
            if r < full_prob+perm_prob:
                # permute
                if width <2: ...
                old_block = a[start:stop].copy()
                kind=rng.random()
                if kind<0.6:
                   shift=int(rng.integers(1, width)); new_block=np.roll(old_block, shift)
                else: new_block=old_block[::-1].copy()
                acc, score_new = _try_block_update(a,c,n,start,stop,new_block)
                if acc: cur_score=score_new; accepted=True
            else:
                # multiplicative block
                old_block = a[start:stop].copy()
                # factor type
                tr=rng.random()
                if tr<0.4:
                   f=float(np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, eta)))
                   new_block=old_block*f
                elif tr<0.7:
                   x=np.linspace(-1,1,width)
                   f=float(np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, eta)))
                   new_block=old_block*np.exp(f*x)
                else:
                   pos=int(rng.integers(1,width)) if width>1 else 1
                   fpos=float(np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, eta)))
                   fneg=float(np.exp(-rng.normal(0.0, eta))) # maybe reciprocal
                   new_block=np.empty(width)
                   new_block[:pos]=old_block[:pos]*fpos
                   new_block[pos:]=old_block[pos:]*fneg
                # occasional noise
                if rng.random()<0.1:
                   beta=float(rng.uniform(0, eta*0.05))*max(1.0, float(old_block.mean()))? For sum1, mean ~1/n small; add 0.001? Better add relative to a.mean(): `eps = a.mean()*float(rng.random()*eta*0.1)`. If eps small.
                   new_block += eps*rng.random(width)
                new_block=np.clip(new_block,0,None)
                acc, score_new = _try_block_update(...)
        if accepted:
           if cur_score < best_score:
              best_score=cur_score; best_a=a.copy()
           stuck=0
        else:
           stuck+=1
        acts+=1
        if acts % 2000 == 0:
            ssum=float(a.sum())
            if ssum <=0 or not np.isfinite(ssum):
                a=np.ones(n)/n; c=_conv(a); cur_score=_score_c(c,n)
            else:
                scale=1/ssum; a*=scale; c*=scale*scale
                # maybe reconvolve every 10000
                if acts % 20000 == 0:
                   c=_conv(a); cur_score=_score_c(c,n)
            if verbose and time.monotonic()-last_print>60:
               print("local n",n,"score",cur_score,"best",best_score)
               last_print=time.monotonic()
        if stuck > (500 if n<256 else 3000):
            # kick
            base = best_a
            prognow = min(1.0, (time.monotonic()-t0)/total_time)
            sigma = 0.45*(0.05/0.45)**prognow + 0.03
            noise = np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, sigma, n))
            a = base * noise
            ok = normalize in place? Use `a, ok = _normalize(a.copy())` returns new.
            c = _conv(a)
            cur_score = _score_c(c,n)
            stuck=0
            if cur_score < best_score: update
    if verbose: print
    return best_a, best_score
```
Need normalize in place? `_normalize` returns new; for kick, we can do:
```
a = base * np.exp(...)
s=a.sum(); if s>0: a/=s else a=ones
```
No need _normalize full.

The accept function top-level:
```
def _accept_move(old_score,new_score,prog,rng):
    if not np.isfinite(new_score): return False
    if new_score <= old_score: return True
    diff = new_score-old_score
    temp = 1e-6*(1-prog)+1e-9
    if diff > 20*temp: return False
    return rng.random() < math.exp(-diff/temp) # import math or np.exp
```
Use `float(np.exp(-diff/temp))`; if exponent underflow. Need diff/temp maybe large yet <=20. Use `np.exp`.

Potential: old_score can be inf? If initial invalid? Then accept finite? local_search initial finite. If old_score inf, `new_score <= inf` true; okay.

In `_try_block_update`, we need if delta max tiny, maybe score same; to save, if no delta, return False with no change? But if move is no change, not accept. We can check `if np.max(np.abs(delta)) < 1e-15: return False, cur_score`. But c backup already? We can check before backup to save.
```
if not np.any(delta): return False, None
```
Need compute `delta` before backup.

`_try_block_update` signature returns `(accepted, score)` and mutates a,c. It needs old_score? Not for restore. It doesn't know acceptance criteria; local_search accepts based on returned score. Now, `_split_sequence`:
```
def _split_sequence(v, rng, noise):
   nv = np.empty(2*v.size, float)
   for i,val in enumerate(v):
       r = float(np.exp(rng.normal(0, noise)))
       # to avoid extreme, clip ratio [0.5,2]
       r = min(2.0, max(0.5, r))
       a = 0.5*val*r
       b = 0.5*val/r
       nv[2*i]=a; nv[2*i+1]=b
   s=nv.sum()
   if s<=0: return np.ones(2*v.size)/(2*v.size)
   return nv/s
```
For B=2 from B=1, this gives [0.25,0.25]*r/ etc. But we can override in hierarchy first with known optimum. Good.

def _sota_lp(p, n, rng, deadline, verbose=False):
    p = _normalize_copy(p); if None: return None,n,inf
    S=np.sqrt(2.0*n)
    f=p*S
    M=float(np.max(_conv(f)))
    best=f.copy(); bestM=M
    no_progress=0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline - 5:
        if M < bestM - 1e-12:
            best=f.copy(); bestM=M; no_progress=0
        else:
            no_progress+=1
            if no_progress>8:
               # perturb and recompute
               f = np.maximum(f, 1e-10); f *= (1 + 0.05*rng.normal(size=n)); normalize to S; M=...
               no_progress=0
        try: b=_lp_update(f,M,rng,n)
        except Exception: b=f.copy()
        if b is None: b=f.copy()
        B=float(np.sum(b))
        if B <=0 or not finite: b=f; B=S
        g=(S/B)*b
        # ensure sum S
        gs=g.sum(); if gs>0: g*=S/gs
        # line search
        cand=None; candM=bestM
        for t in (0.5,0.25,0.1,0.05,0.02,0.01,0.005):
            c=(1-t)*f + t*g
            c=np.maximum(c,1e-10)
            s=c.sum(); if s<=0: continue
            c=s? normalize to S: c*=S/s
            cm=float(np.max(_conv(c)))
            if cm <= M + 1e-9: # non-increase
                cand=c; candM=cm; break
        if cand is not None:
            f=cand; M=candM
            if M < bestM: best=f; bestM=M; no_progress=0
        else:
            # try smallest t maybe if improves bestM? consider if any t significantly lower?
            for t in (0.001,0.0005): ... if cm < M - 1e-6: accept
    pbest = best / S
    return pbest, n, bestM
```

```
def _lp_update(f,M,rng,n):
   if linprog is None: return None
   A=np.zeros((2*n-1,n),float)
   for j in range(n): A[j:j+n,j]=f
   bub=np.full(2*n-1, M*(1+1e-9)+1e-12)
   coef=-np.ones(n); coef -= 1e-9*rng.random(n)
   bounds=[(0.0,None)]*n
   res=linprog(coef, A_ub=A, b_ub=bub, bounds=bounds, method='highs', options={'presolve': True})
   if res.success and res.x is not None:
      b=np.asarray(res.x,float); b[b<0]=0; return b
   return None
```

def _repeat_to(p,n):
   m=p.size; base = n//m; rem = n%m
   reps=np.full(m,base); reps[:rem]+=1
   q=np.repeat(p, reps)
   s=q.sum(); q/=s
   return q
```

Detailed `_try_block_update` implementation with local variables and c_stop:
```
def _try_block_update(a,c,n,start,stop,new_block,old_score,prog,rng):
    width = stop-start
    if width <=0: return None
    old_block = a[start:stop].copy()
    delta = new_block - old_block
    if delta.size ==0: return None
    maxd = float(np.max(np.abs(delta)))
    if not np.isfinite(maxd) or maxd < 1e-15:
        return None
    c_start = start
    c_stop = min(2*n - 1, n + stop - 1) # exclusive? If stop=n, c_stop=2n-1 good. For start=0,stop=1 exclusive n, affected indices 0..n-1 yes c[0:n]. pair selfconv off0 len1 c[0] included. Good.
    c_bak = c[c_start:c_stop].copy()
    aa = a; cc=c; nn=n
    # linear
    for off in range(width):
        d = float(delta[off])
        if d != 0.0:
            idx = start + off
            cc[idx:idx+nn] += (2.0 * d) * aa
    sc = np.convolve(delta, delta)
    off = 2*start
    cc[off:off+2*width-1] += sc
    a[start:stop] = new_block
    block_diff = float(np.sum(new_block) - np.sum(old_block))
    S_new = 1.0 + block_diff
    if not np.isfinite(S_new) or S_new <= 1e-12:
        c[c_start:c_stop] = c_bak; a[start:stop]=old_block; return None
    cmax = float(np.max(cc))
    if not np.isfinite(cmax) or cmax < 0.0: # cmax negative? use 0
        c_max0 = 0.0 if cmax <0 else cmax
    else: c_max0=cmax
    score = (2.0 * nn * c_max0) / (S_new * S_new)
    if not np.isfinite(score): restore
    if _accept_move(old_score, score, prog, rng):
        if S_new != 1.0:
            scale = 1.0 / S_new
            a *= scale
            c *= (scale * scale)
        return score
    c[c_start:c_stop]=c_bak
    a[start:stop]=old_block
    return None
```
Cmax negative possible due to drift; if all c negative? set 0 -> score0 would falsely accept. But should not. If cmax<0 due to catastrophic, reconvolve needed; we can reject if cmax<0. Use `if cmax < 0: cmax=0.0` but score0 might accept and ruin. Better if cmax < 1e-30, reject? For valid nonnegative cmax>0. We can restore if cmax <0. Do that.
`score` should be compared. If S_new scaling changes cmax; cur_score after accept is score (same as 2n*max(c_scaled)). Good.

But if a sum not exactly1 due to previous scaling drift, S_new formula using 1 may be slightly off. Score scale difference 1e-12. Fine.

Potential c slice affected by pair selfconv at off maybe < c_stop? off=2start, off+len-1=2start+2w-2. c_stop = n+stop-1 = n+start+w-1. Since n>=start+w? stop<=n, so n+start+w-1 >= 2start+2w-2 iff n >= start+w+1? If start+w=n, c_stop=2n-1 > max pair; okay. If start=0,w=n, c_stop=2n-1. Good.

`_accept_move`: 
```
def _accept_move(old,new,prog,rng):
    if not np.isfinite(new): return False
    if old is None or not np.isfinite(old): return True
    if new <= old: return True
    diff = new-old
    temp = 1e-6*(1.0-prog)+1e-10
    if diff <= 0: return True
    if diff > 100*temp: return False
    return float(rng.random()) < float(np.exp(-diff/temp))
```
Temp 1e-6 score differences; near target, cmax differences 1e-9 score diff 2n*1e-9 ~5e-6? Actually if cmax diff 1e-9, score diff ~5e-6. Temp too small. Use temp start 1e-5? At score 1.5, diff 1e-5 relative 1e-5. Near optimum improvements maybe 1e-7. We need allow small. Set temp = 5e-6*(1-prog)+1e-9. Max diff accepted exp ~ if diff/temp>50 reject. 100*temp 5e-4 too large? We can set if diff>50*temp reject. This allows very small. Use `if diff > 50*temp: return False`. Exponent -diff/temp.

But if temp 1e-8 late, diff 1e-8 accepted prob 0.6. Good.

For early coarse hierarchy, temp small but deterministic accept handles.

def _get_parent():
  obj = globals().get('height_sequence_1', None)
  if obj is None: return None
  arr = np.asarray(obj, dtype=np.float64)
  if arr.ndim !=1: arr=arr.ravel()
  if arr.size==0: return None
  arr=np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)

`_full_score`:
```
def _full_score(a):
  p=_normalize_array(a)
  if p is None: return np.inf
  n=p.size
  if n==0: return np.inf
  c=np.convolve(p,p)
  if c.size==0: return np.inf
  return float(2.0*n*np.max(c))
```
If p all zeros? normalize None.

`_score_c`:
```
def _score_c(c,n):
  if c.size==0: return np.inf
  m=float(np.max(c))
  if not np.isfinite(m): return np.inf
  if m<0: m=0.0
  return float(2.0*n*m)
```
Since normalized sum1.

`_accept_move` as above.

`_global_smooth`:
```
def _global_smooth(a,n,rng,eta):
  r=float(rng.random())
  if n<=1: return None
  if r<0.25:
    phi=np.linspace(-1.0,1.0,n)
  elif r<0.45:
    phi=np.linspace(1.0,-1.0,n)
  elif r<0.65:
    x=np.linspace(-1.0,1.0,n); phi=x*x-0.333333
  else:
    f=int(rng.integers(1,6))
    ph=float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0*np.pi))
    x=np.linspace(0.0, 2.0*np.pi, n, endpoint=False)
    phi=np.sin(float(f)*x + ph)
  if not np.any(np.isfinite(phi)): return None
  new=a*np.exp(float(eta)*phi)
  return _normalize_array(new)
```
`np.exp` with eta small, phi range -1..1, okay.

In update, after pair c, old_block sum: `old_sum_block = float(np.sum(old_block))` before? We compute block_diff after copy.

```
def _try_block_update(...):
  width = stop-start
  if width <= 0: return None
  old_block = a[start:stop].copy()
  new_block = np.asarray(new_block, dtype=np.float64)
  if new_block.size != width: return None
  new_block = np.where(np.isfinite(new_block), new_block, 0.0)
  new_block = np.maximum(new_block, 0.0)
  delta = new_block - old_block
  maxd = float(np.max(np.abs(delta))) if delta.size else 0.0
  if not np.isfinite(maxd) or maxd < 1e-15: return None
  c_start = start
  c_stop = min(2*n-1, n + stop - 1)
  c_bak = c[c_start:c_stop].copy()
  aa=a; cc=c; nn=n
  for off in range(width):
     d=float(delta[off])
     if d != 0.0:
        idx=start+off
        cc[idx:idx+nn] += (2.0*d)*aa
  sc=np.convolve(delta, delta)
  offp=2*start
  if offp + sc.size <= cc.size:
      cc[offp:offp+sc.size] += sc
  a[start:stop]=new_block
  snew=1.0 + float(np.sum(new_block) - np.sum(old_block))
  if not np.isfinite(snew) or snew <=1e-12:
      c[c_start:c_stop]=c_bak; a[start:stop]=old_block; return None
  cmax=float(np.max(cc))
  if not np.isfinite(cmax) or cmax < 0.0:
      c[c_start:c_stop]=c_bak; a[start:stop]=old_block; return None
  score=float(2.0*nn*cmax/(snew*snew))
  if not np.isfinite(score): restore
  if _accept_move(old_score, score, prog, rng):
      if snew != 1.0:
         scale=1.0/snew; a*=scale; c*=scale*scale
      return score
  c[c_start:c_stop]=c_bak; a[start:stop]=old_block
  return None
```
If cmax==0? score0 invalid; but if all zeros? cmax0 and snew positive maybe sequence all zeros. Reject if cmax <=0? For valid sequence cmax>0. If a has support one point? cmax>0. Use if cmax <=0.0 restore. But if n large and some underflow? unlikely. Use `<0` okay. score0 could be accepted if cmax tiny positive? no. Use if cmax <= 1e-300 reject.

if tr<0.35:
   factor=float(np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, eta)))
   new_block=old_block*factor
elif tr<0.65:
   x=np.linspace(-1.0,1.0,width)
   slope=float(rng.normal(0.0, eta))
   new_block=old_block*np.exp(slope*x)
else:
   split=int(rng.integers(1,width)) if width>1 else 1
   f1=float(np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, eta)))
   f2=float(np.exp(-float(rng.normal(0.0, eta)))) # f2 positive independent
   new_block=np.empty(width)

   new_block[split:]=old_block[split:]*f2
if rng.random()<0.15:
   mfloat=float(np.mean(old_block)) if width else 0.0
   baseamp=max(1e-12, mfloat)*float(rng.uniform(0.0, eta*0.2))
   new_block = new_block + baseamp*rng.random(width)
new_block=np.clip(new_block,0.0,None)

def _sota_lp(p, n, rng, deadline, verbose=False):
    p=_normalize_array(p)
    if p is None: return None, n, np.inf
    if n != p.size: p=_interpolate_to(p,n)
    S=float(np.sqrt(2.0*n))
    f=p*S
    M=_score_c(np.convolve(f,f),n)  # score
    best=f.copy(); bestM=M
    no=0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline - 8:
       if M < bestM - 1e-10:
          best=f.copy(); bestM=M; no=0
       else:
          no += 1
          if no > 6:
             f = f * (1.0 + 0.05*rng.normal(size=n))
             f = np.maximum(f, 1e-10)
             s=f.sum()
             if s<=0: break
             f *= S/s
             M=_score_c(np.convolve(f,f),n)
             no=0
       b=_lp_update(f,M,rng,n)
       if b is None:
          b=f.copy()
       Bb=float(np.sum(b))
       if not np.isfinite(Bb) or Bb <= 1e-12:
          g=f.copy()
       else:
          g=(S/Bb)*b
          gs=float(np.sum(g))
          if gs <=0 or not finite: g=f.copy()
          else: g*=S/gs
       g=np.maximum(g,1e-10); gs=g.sum(); if gs>0: g*=S/gs
       cand=None; candM=M
       for t in (0.5,0.25,0.1,0.05,0.02,0.01,0.005):
          c=(1-t)*f + t*g
          c=np.maximum(c,1e-10)
          s=float(np.sum(c))
          if s<=0: continue
          c*=S/s
          cm=_score_c(np.convolve(c,c),n)
          if cm <= M + 1e-9:
             cand=c; candM=cm; break
       if cand is None:
          for t in (0.001,0.0005):
             c=... if cm < M -1e-7: accept
       if cand is not None:
          f=cand; M=candM
          if M < bestM -1e-12:
             best=f.copy(); bestM=M; no=0
       else:
          no +=1
    pbest=best/S
    return pbest, n, float(bestM)

```python
"""
Hierarchical multi-resolution local search for nonnegative step functions.

The algorithm builds sequences by progressively splitting blocks and polishing
each scale with a fast incremental-convolution local search.  It also uses the
linear-programming style "covering" update from the previous state of the art
on small scales, and finally refines several upscaled candidates at the target
resolution.  The objective is homogeneous, so all internal sequences are kept
with sum 1 and scored as 2*n*max(convolve(p,p)).
"""

import time
import operator
import numpy as np

try:
    from scipy.optimize import linprog
except Exception:
    linprog = None


def _normalize_array(a):
    """Return a finite nonnegative 1D float array with sum 1, or None."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.ndim != 1:
        a = a.ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return None
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        return None
    return a / s


def _get_parent():
    """Fetch and normalize the global height_sequence_1 if available."""
    obj = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
    if obj is None:
        return None
    a = np.asarray(obj, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.ndim != 1:
        a = a.ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return None
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        return None
    return a / s


def _full_score(a):
    """Score of an arbitrary sequence under the same formula (sum normalized)."""
    p = _normalize_array(a)
    if p is None:
        return np.inf
    n = p.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.inf
    c = np.convolve(p, p)
    if c.size == 0:
        return np.inf
    return float(2.0 * n * np.max(c))


def _score_c(c, n):
    """Score from convolution of a sum-1 sequence."""
    if c.size == 0:
        return np.inf
    m = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(m):
        return np.inf
    if m < 0.0:
        m = 0.0
    return float(2.0 * n * m)


def _accept_move(old_score, new_score, prog, rng):
    """Deterministic hill climbing with a tiny annealing temperature."""
    if not np.isfinite(new_score):
        return False
    if old_score is None or not np.isfinite(old_score):
        return True
    if new_score <= old_score:
        return True
    diff = float(new_score - old_score)
    if diff <= 0.0:
        return True
    temp = 5e-6 * (1.0 - float(prog)) + 1e-10
    if diff > 50.0 * temp:
        return False
    return float(rng.random()) < float(np.exp(-diff / temp))


def _global_smooth(a, n, rng, eta):
    """Global smooth multiplicative perturbation."""
    if n <= 1:
        return None
    r = float(rng.random())
    if r < 0.25:
        phi = np.linspace(-1.0, 1.0, n)
    elif r < 0.45:
        phi = np.linspace(1.0, -1.0, n)
    elif r < 0.65:
        x = np.linspace(-1.0, 1.0, n)
        phi = x * x - 0.3333333333333333
    else:
        f = int(rng.integers(1, 6))
        ph = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
        x = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi, n, endpoint=False)
        phi = np.sin(float(f) * x + ph)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(phi)):
        return None
    new_a = a * np.exp(float(eta) * phi)
    return _normalize_array(new_a)


def _try_block_update(a, c, n, start, stop, new_block, old_score, prog, rng):
    """
    Apply a block change to a and its convolution c incrementally.

    Returns the new score if accepted, otherwise None.  If rejected, a and c
    are restored.
    """
    width = stop - start
    if width <= 0 or n <= 0:
        return None

    old_block = a[start:stop].copy()
    new_block = np.asarray(new_block, dtype=np.float64)
    if new_block.size != width:
        return None
    new_block = np.where(np.isfinite(new_block), new_block, 0.0)
    new_block = np.maximum(new_block, 0.0)

    delta = new_block - old_block
    maxd = float(np.max(np.abs(delta))) if delta.size else 0.0
    if not np.isfinite(maxd) or maxd < 1e-15:
        return None

    c_start = start
    c_stop = min(2 * n - 1, n + stop - 1)
    c_backup = c[c_start:c_stop].copy()

    aa = a
    cc = c
    nn = n

    # Linear part: 2 * convolve(delta_full, old_a)
    for off in range(width):
        d = float(delta[off])
        if d != 0.0:
            idx = start + off
            cc[idx:idx + nn] += (2.0 * d) * aa

    # Quadratic part: convolve(delta, delta)
    self_conv = np.convolve(delta, delta)
    offp = 2 * start
    if offp + self_conv.size <= cc.size:
        cc[offp:offp + self_conv.size] += self_conv

    a[start:stop] = new_block
    s_new = 1.0 + float(np.sum(new_block) - np.sum(old_block))

    if not np.isfinite(s_new) or s_new <= 1e-12:
        c[c_start:c_stop] = c_backup
        a[start:stop] = old_block
        return None

    cmax = float(np.max(cc))
    if not np.isfinite(cmax) or cmax <= 1e-300:
        c[c_start:c_stop] = c_backup
        a[start:stop] = old_block
        return None

    score = float(2.0 * nn * cmax / (s_new * s_new))
    if not np.isfinite(score):
        c[c_start:c_stop] = c_backup
        a[start:stop] = old_block
        return None

    if _accept_move(old_score, score, prog, rng):
        if s_new != 1.0:
            scale = 1.0 / s_new
            a *= scale
            c *= (scale * scale)
        return score

    c[c_start:c_stop] = c_backup
    a[start:stop] = old_block
    return None


def _local_search(initial, n, rng, deadline, verbose=False):
    """
    Fast local search with incremental convolution updates, kicks, and
    global smooth moves.
    """
    a = _normalize_array(initial)
    if a is None or a.size != n:
        a = _normalize_array(_interpolate_to(initial, n)) if initial is not None else np.ones(n) / float(n)
    if a is None:
        a = np.ones(n) / float(n)
    if a.size != n:
        a = _interpolate_to(a, n)
        a = _normalize_array(a)
        if a is None:
            a = np.ones(n) / float(n)

    c = np.convolve(a, a)
    cur_score = _score_c(c, n)
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_score = float(cur_score)

    t0 = time.monotonic()
    total_time = max(1e-12, float(deadline - t0))
    if total_time <= 1.0:
        return best_a, best_score

    acts = 0
    stuck = 0
    last_print = t0

    while time.monotonic() < deadline - 1.0:
        elapsed = time.monotonic() - t0
        prog = min(1.0, elapsed / total_time)
        eta = 0.80 * (0.03 / 0.80) ** prog

        if n <= 64:
            full_prob = 0.28
            perm_prob = 0.18
        else:
            full_prob = 0.12
            perm_prob = 0.15

        r = float(rng.random())
        accepted = False

        if r < full_prob:
            new_a = _global_smooth(a, n, rng, 0.65 * eta)
            if new_a is not None:
                c_new = np.convolve(new_a, new_a)
                score_new = _score_c(c_new, n)
                if _accept_move(cur_score, score_new, prog, rng):
                    a = new_a
                    c = c_new
                    cur_score = score_new
                    accepted = True
        else:
            if n <= 64:
                wlo = 1
                whi = max(2, n // 2)
            else:
                wlo = max(2, min(12, n // 250))
                whi = max(wlo + 1, min(n // 8, 128))
            whi = min(whi, n)
            width = int(rng.integers(wlo, whi + 1))
            if width <= 0 or width > n:
                width = n
            if width == 1:
                start = int(rng.integers(0, n))
            else:
                start = int(rng.integers(0, max(1, n - width + 1)))
            stop = start + width

            if width == 1:
                # single-coordinate multiplicative change is too weak; use a small block
                start2 = max(0, start - 2)
                stop2 = min(n, start + 3)
                start, stop = start2, stop2
                width = stop - start

            if r < full_prob + perm_prob:
                if width < 2:
                    continue
                old_block = a[start:stop].copy()
                if float(rng.random()) < 0.65:
                    shift = int(rng.integers(1, width))
                    new_block = np.roll(old_block, shift)
                else:
                    new_block = old_block[::-1].copy()
                score_new = _try_block_update(
                    a, c, n, start, stop, new_block, cur_score, prog, rng
                )
                if score_new is not None:
                    cur_score = float(score_new)
                    accepted = True
            else:
                old_block = a[start:stop].copy()
                tr = float(rng.random())
                if tr < 0.35:
                    factor = float(np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, eta)))
                    new_block = old_block * factor
                elif tr < 0.65:
                    if width == 1:
                        x = np.array([0.0])
                    else:
                        x = np.linspace(-1.0, 1.0, width)
                    slope = float(rng.normal(0.0, eta))
                    new_block = old_block * np.exp(slope * x)
                else:
                    if width <= 1:
                        split = 1
                    else:
                        split = int(rng.integers(1, width))
                    f1 = float(np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, eta)))
                    f2 = float(np.exp(-float(rng.normal(0.0, eta))))
                    new_block = np.empty(width, dtype=np.float64)
                    new_block[:split] = old_block[:split] * f1
                    new_block[split:] = old_block[split:] * f2

                if float(rng.random()) < 0.15:
                    mfloat = float(np.mean(old_block)) if width else 0.0
                    base_amp = max(1e-12, mfloat) * float(rng.uniform(0.0, 0.25 * max(0.05, eta)))
                    new_block = new_block + base_amp * rng.random(width)

                new_block = np.maximum(new_block, 0.0)
                score_new = _try_block_update(
                    a, c, n, start, stop, new_block, cur_score, prog, rng
                )
                if score_new is not None:
                    cur_score = float(score_new)
                    accepted = True

        if accepted:
            if cur_score < best_score:
                best_score = float(cur_score)
                best_a = a.copy()
            stuck = 0
        else:
            stuck += 1

        acts += 1
        if acts % 4000 == 0:
            ssum = float(a.sum())
            if not np.isfinite(ssum) or ssum <= 1e-300:
                a = np.ones(n) / float(n)
                c = np.convolve(a, a)
                cur_score = _score_c(c, n)
            else:
                scale = 1.0 / ssum
                a *= scale
                c *= (scale * scale)
                cur_score = _score_c(c, n)
                if acts % 30000 == 0 or abs(ssum - 1.0) > 1e-4:
                    c = np.convolve(a, a)
                    cur_score = _score_c(c, n)

            if verbose:
                now = time.monotonic()
                if now - last_print > 45.0:
                    print("  local n=%d score=%.8f best=%.8f actions=%d" % (
                        n, cur_score, best_score, acts
                    ))
                    last_print = now

        if stuck > (400 if n < 256 else 2500):
            base = best_a
            sigma = 0.45 * (0.05 / 0.45) ** prog + 0.03
            noise = np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, sigma, n))
            a = base * noise
            ssum = float(a.sum())
            if np.isfinite(ssum) and ssum > 1e-300:
                a *= 1.0 / ssum
            else:
                a = np.ones(n) / float(n)
            c = np.convolve(a, a)
            cur_score = _score_c(c, n)
            stuck = 0
            if cur_score < best_score:
                best_score = float(cur_score)
                best_a = a.copy()

    return best_a, float(best_score)


def _split_sequence(v, rng, noise):
    """Split each coordinate into two nearby siblings, preserving sum."""
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64)
    m = v.size
    nv = np.empty(2 * m, dtype=np.float64)
    for i in range(m):
        val = float(v[i])
        if val <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(val):
            nv[2 * i] = 0.5
            nv[2 * i + 1] = 0.5
            continue
        r = float(np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, float(noise))))
        r = min(2.0, max(0.5, r))
        nv[2 * i] = 0.5 * val * r
        nv[2 * i + 1] = 0.5 * val / r
    s = float(nv.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        return np.ones(2 * m) / float(2 * m)
    return nv / s


def _hierarchical_build(max_n, deadline, rng):
    """
    Build sequences at increasing block resolutions, polishing each scale.
    Returns a list of (sequence, length, score).
    """
    seqs = []
    current = np.array([1.0], dtype=np.float64)

    levels = [
        (2, 1.0),
        (4, 2.0),
        (8, 3.0),
        (16, 5.0),
        (32, 10.0),
        (64, 20.0),
        (128, 35.0),
        (256, 60.0),
        (512, 90.0),
        (1024, 120.0),
        (2048, 160.0),
    ]

    for b, tsec in levels:
        if b > max_n:
            break
        now = time.monotonic()
        if now >= deadline - 3.0:
            break
        level_deadline = min(deadline - 2.0, now + float(tsec))
        if level_deadline - now < 0.8:
            break

        if b == 2:
            new = np.array([1.0 / 3.0, 2.0 / 3.0], dtype=np.float64)
        else:
            new = _split_sequence(current, rng, 0.25)

        best_p, best_s = _local_search(new, b, rng, level_deadline, verbose=False)
        if best_p is not None and np.isfinite(best_s):
            seqs.append((best_p.copy(), b, float(best_s)))
            current = best_p
        else:
            current = new

    return seqs


def _lp_update(f, m, rng, n):
    """
    Solve the auxiliary linear program:
      max sum(b)  s.t. f*b <= m, b >= 0.
    Returns b or None.
    """
    if linprog is None or n > 512:
        return None
    a_ub = np.zeros((2 * n - 1, n), dtype=np.float64)
    for j in range(n):
        a_ub[j:j + n, j] = f

    b_ub = np.full(2 * n - 1, float(m) * (1.0 + 1e-9) + 1e-12, dtype=np.float64)
    coef = -np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    coef -= 1e-9 * rng.random(n)
    bounds = [(0.0, None)] * n

    try:
        res = linprog(
            coef,
            A_ub=a_ub,
            b_ub=b_ub,
            bounds=bounds,
            method="highs",
            options={"presolve": True}
        )
    except Exception:
        return None

    if res is not None and res.success and res.x is not None:
        b = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
        b = np.where(np.isfinite(b), b, 0.0)
        b = np.maximum(b, 0.0)
        return b
    return None


def _sota_lp(p, n, rng, deadline):
    """
    Apply the previous state-of-the-art alternating LP/convex combination
    iteration on a sum-1 sequence.  Returns (p_best, n, score).
    """
    p = _normalize_array(p)
    if p is None:
        return None, n, np.inf
    if p.size != n:
        p = _interpolate_to(p, n)
        p = _normalize_array(p)
        if p is None:
            return None, n, np.inf

    s_norm = float(np.sqrt(2.0 * n))
    f = p * s_norm
    c = np.convolve(f, f)
    m = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(m):
        return None, n, np.inf

    best_f = f.copy()
    best_m = m
    no_progress = 0

    while time.monotonic() < deadline - 8.0:
        if m < best_m - 1e-10:
            best_f = f.copy()
            best_m = m
            no_progress = 0
        else:
            no_progress += 1
            if no_progress > 6:
                f = f * (1.0 + 0.05 * rng.normal(size=n))
                f = np.maximum(f, 1e-10)
                ssum = float(f.sum())
                if not np.isfinite(ssum) or ssum <= 1e-300:
                    break
                f *= s_norm / ssum
                c = np.convolve(f, f)
                m = float(np.max(c))
                if not np.isfinite(m):
                    break
                no_progress = 0

        b = _lp_update(f, m, rng, n)
        if b is None:
            b = f.copy()

        bsum = float(b.sum())
        if not np.isfinite(bsum) or bsum <= 1e-300:
            g = f.copy()
        else:
            g = (s_norm / bsum) * b
            gsum = float(g.sum())
            if not np.isfinite(gsum) or gsum <= 1e-300:
                g = f.copy()
            else:
                g *= s_norm / gsum

        g = np.maximum(g, 1e-10)
        gsum = float(g.sum())
        if gsum > 1e-300:
            g *= s_norm / gsum

        cand = None
        cand_m = m
        for t in (0.5, 0.25, 0.1, 0.05, 0.02, 0.01, 0.005):
            cand_v = (1.0 - t) * f + t * g
            cand_v = np.maximum(cand_v, 1e-10)
            csum = float(cand_v.sum())
            if not np.isfinite(csum) or csum <= 1e-300:
                continue
            cand_v *= s_norm / csum
            cm = float(np.max(np.convolve(cand_v, cand_v)))
            if np.isfinite(cm) and cm <= m + 1e-9:
                cand = cand_v
                cand_m = cm
                break

        if cand is None:
            for t in (0.001, 0.0005):
                cand_v = (1.0 - t) * f + t * g
                cand_v = np.maximum(cand_v, 1e-10)
                csum = float(cand_v.sum())
                if not np.isfinite(csum) or csum <= 1e-300:
                    continue
                cand_v *= s_norm / csum
                cm = float(np.max(np.convolve(cand_v, cand_v)))
                if np.isfinite(cm) and cm < m - 1e-7:
                    cand = cand_v
                    cand_m = cm
                    break

        if cand is not None:
            f = cand
            m = cand_m
            if m < best_m - 1e-12:
                best_f = f.copy()
                best_m = m
                no_progress = 0
        else:
            no_progress += 1

    if not np.isfinite(best_m):
        return None, n, np.inf
    p_best = best_f / s_norm
    p_best = _normalize_array(p_best)
    if p_best is None:
        return None, n, np.inf
    return p_best, n, float(best_m)


def _interpolate_to(p, n):
    """Linear interpolation to length n, normalized to sum 1."""
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    m = p.size
    if m == 0:
        return np.ones(n) / float(n)
    if m == 1:
        return np.ones(n) / float(n)
    if m == n:
        p = np.where(np.isfinite(p), p, 0.0)
        p = np.maximum(p, 0.0)
        s = float(p.sum())
        if s <= 1e-300:
            return np.ones(n) / float(n)
        return p / s

    x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, m)
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    q = np.interp(x_new, x_old, p)
    q = np.where(np.isfinite(q), q, 0.0)
    q = np.maximum(q, 0.0)
    s = float(q.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        return np.ones(n) / float(n)
    return q / s


def _repeat_to(p, n):
    """Block-repeat to length n, normalized to sum 1."""
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    m = p.size
    if m == 0:
        return np.ones(n) / float(n)
    base = n // m
    rem = n % m
    reps = np.full(m, base, dtype=np.int64)
    if rem > 0:
        reps[:rem] += 1
    q = np.repeat(p, reps)
    q = np.where(np.isfinite(q), q, 0.0)
    q = np.maximum(q, 0.0)
    s = float(q.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        return np.ones(n) / float(n)
    return q / s


def _smooth_candidate(a):
    """Small box smoothing."""
    p = _normalize_array(a)
    if p is None:
        return None
    k = 5
    kernel = np.ones(k, dtype=np.float64) / float(k)
    q = np.convolve(p, kernel, mode="same")
    q = np.where(np.isfinite(q), q, 0.0)
    q = np.maximum(q, 0.0)
    s = float(q.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        return None
    return q / s


def _add_candidate(base_list, a, tag):
    """Add a normalized candidate and its score to base_list."""
    p = _normalize_array(a)
    if p is None:
        return
    sc = _full_score(p)
    if np.isfinite(sc):
        base_list.append((p, float(sc), tag))


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    """Search for low-scoring nonnegative step-function heights."""
    try:
        seed = int(seed)
    except Exception:
        seed = 42
    try:
        budget_s = float(budget_s)
    except Exception:
        budget_s = 2400.0
    if not np.isfinite(budget_s) or budget_s < 60.0:
        budget_s = 60.0

    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    t_start = time.monotonic()
    hard_deadline = t_start + max(0.0, budget_s) - 10.0

    best_a = None
    best_score = np.inf

    parent = _get_parent()
    if parent is not None:
        scp = _full_score(parent)
        if np.isfinite(scp) and scp < best_score:
            best_a = parent.copy()
            best_score = float(scp)

    n_final = 2535

    # --- multi-resolution construction ---
    hierarchy_time = min(max(60.0, 0.30 * budget_s), max(60.0, budget_s - 1700.0))
    hierarchy_deadline = min(hard_deadline - 1400.0, t_start + hierarchy_time)
    hierarchy_deadline = max(t_start + 10.0, hierarchy_deadline)
    print("hierarchy deadline seconds remaining: %.1f" % max(0.0, hierarchy_deadline - t_start))

    smalls = _hierarchical_build(2048, hierarchy_deadline, rng)
    if smalls:
        smalls.sort(key=operator.itemgetter(2))
        for pp, nn, ss in smalls:
            if np.isfinite(ss) and ss < best_score:
                best_score = float(ss)
                best_a = pp.copy()

    # --- small-scale LP polish ---
    if linprog is not None and smalls:
        lp_item = None
        for pp, nn, ss in smalls:
            if nn <= 512 and np.isfinite(ss):
                if lp_item is None or nn > lp_item[1] or (nn == lp_item[1] and ss < lp_item[2]):
                    lp_item = (pp, nn, ss)

        if lp_item is not None:
            lp_deadline = min(hard_deadline - 1300.0, time.monotonic() + 90.0)
            if lp_deadline > time.monotonic() + 15.0:
                pp, nn, ss = _sota_lp(lp_item[0], lp_item[1], rng, lp_deadline)
                if pp is not None and np.isfinite(ss):
                    smalls.append((pp, nn, float(ss)))
                    if ss < best_score:
                        best_score = float(ss)
                        best_a = pp.copy()
                    print("LP polish n=%d score=%.8f" % (nn, ss))

    # --- final candidate bases ---
    if parent is not None:
        if parent.size <= 200000:
            _add_candidate(base_list := [], parent, "parent")
            if parent.size != n_final:
                _add_candidate(base_list, _interpolate_to(parent, n_final), "parent_interp")

    # ensure base_list exists even if parent absent
    base_list = globals().get("__base_list_placeholder", None)
    if base_list is None:
        base_list = []

    # The above block used a temporary local list; rebuild cleanly to avoid globals:
    base_list = []
    if parent is not None:
        if parent.size <= 200000:
            _add_candidate(base_list, parent, "parent")
            if parent.size != n_final:
                _add_candidate(base_list, _interpolate_to(parent, n_final), "parent_interp")

    _add_candidate(base_list, np.ones(n_final, dtype=np.float64), "uniform")

    if smalls:
        valids = []
        for pp, nn, ss in smalls:
            if pp is not None and np.isfinite(ss):
                valids.append((pp, nn, float(ss)))
        if valids:
            valids.sort(key=operator.itemgetter(2))
            top_two = valids[:2]
            max_item = valids[0]
            for item in valids:
                if item[1] > max_item[1]:
                    max_item = item

            chosen = top_two
            if max_item not in chosen:
                chosen.append(max_item)

            for item in chosen:
                pp, nn, ss = item
                qint = _interpolate_to(pp, n_final)
                _add_candidate(base_list, qint, "small%d_interp" % nn)
                qrep = _repeat_to(pp, n_final)
                _add_candidate(base_list, qrep, "small%d_repeat" % nn)
                qsm = _smooth_candidate(qint)
                if qsm is not None:
                    _add_candidate(base_list, qsm, "small%d_smooth" % nn)

    if not base_list:
        base_list = [(np.ones(n_final) / float(n_final), 2.0, "uniform")]

    base_list.sort(key=operator.itemgetter(1))

    selected = []
    used_tags = set()
    for item in base_list:
        tag = item[2]
        if tag in used_tags:
            continue
        used_tags.add(tag)
        selected.append(item)
        if len(selected) >= 3:
            break

    # --- final high-resolution refinement ---
    final_deadline = hard_deadline - 15.0
    if selected and time.monotonic() < final_deadline - 45.0:
        total_final = max(45.0, final_deadline - time.monotonic())
        slice_time = total_final / float(max(1, len(selected)))
        print("final candidates:", len(selected), "slice_time %.1f" % slice_time)

        for idx, (a0, s0, tag) in enumerate(selected):
            end = min(final_deadline - 12.0, time.monotonic() + slice_time)
            if end - time.monotonic() < 25.0:
                continue
            if best_score < np.inf and s0 > best_score + 0.25 and idx > 0:
                a0 = _interpolate_to(best_a, n_final)
                s0 = _full_score(a0)
                tag = tag + "_from_best"
            a1, s1 = _local_search(a0, n_final, rng, end, verbose=True)
            if a1 is not None and np.isfinite(s1):
                if s1 < best_score:
                    best_score = float(s1)
                    best_a = a1.copy()
            print("final base %s -> %.8f, global best=%.8f" % (tag, s1 if a1 is not None else np.nan, best_score))

    # --- finalize ---
    if best_a is None:
        best_a = np.ones(n_final, dtype=np.float64) / float(n_final)

    best_a = _normalize_array(best_a)
    if best_a is None:
        best_a = np.ones(n_final, dtype=np.float64) / float(n_final)

    # authoritative check and fallback if something invalid slipped through
    evalfunc = globals().get("evaluate_sequence", None)
    final_list = [float(x) for x in best_a]
    if callable(evalfunc) and time.monotonic() < hard_deadline - 2.0:
        try:
            fs = float(evalfunc(final_list))
            if not np.isfinite(fs):
                fallback = [float(x) for x in np.ones(n_final) / float(n_final)]
                try:
                    fs2 = float(evalfunc(fallback))
                    if np.isfinite(fs2):
                        final_list = fallback
                except Exception:
                    pass
        except Exception:
            pass

    return final_list
```
